Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the classical_mechanics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 698 and the convergence rate is O(460/n^5).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the classical_mechanics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 698 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 460. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Theorem 213.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Given a classical_mechanics algebra A over field F with optimality condition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 595. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 932/5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 595 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the classical_mechanics structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 932. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
